<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A real time clock that provides an accurate and always up to date readout of time. The clock value changes even if it's far away from the player. Zero value of the clock is at the point when the game started. You cannot reset the clock, it always tells the time since the game started. The clock outputs time via 5 outputs, each of them 4-bit analog signal. Sixteen least significant bits are on the four sides, and the 5th output (containing the most significant 4 bits) is on the back (you must place the clock on a wire-through-block to access the 5th output). The clock is set up so that it advances by exactly 4096 ticks each day. That means each tick is about 0.3 seconds long and that 4th output contains number of days since the game started. Combined value of outputs 1, 2 and 3 contains time of day, to about 0.3 second accuracy. 4th output alone will overflow after 16 days. Use 5th output to extend the overflow period to 256 days. 
</div>

<h3 id='S'>S</h3>

<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>SR Latch</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SRLatchBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>241</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Logic SR latch. Can be used as a memory element holding a single bit. Has three inputs (S, R and clock), and one output. A signal on S input sets the state to 1. A signal on R input resets the state to 0. Output always indicates the current state of the latch. Can work in synchronous or asynchronous mode, depending on whether clock input is connected. In synchronous mode the states of S and R inputs are read only during a rising edge on the clock input. Can be placed on any surface and rotated to desired orientation.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Saddle</strong></td>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Saddle makes it possible to ride horses and other animals. A horse without a saddle will quickly dump the rider and run away.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Saltpeter Chunk</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SaltpeterChunkBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>33</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A chunk of potassium nitrate. This is a white substance with high oxygen content that can be used as an oxidizer for making explosives or, when sprinkled over soil, as a fertilizer to speed up growth of plants and increase yields.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Saltpeter Ore</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SaltpeterOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>28</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Saltpeter ore is a vein of potassium nitrate embedded in sandstone rock. Gains saltpeter chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost. Saltpeter can be used to make explosives.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sand</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SandBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>13</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sand is a yellowish loose material that covers desert areas and beaches. Be careful because sand will collapse when tunnelled under. It is most commonly used to make glass. Cacti will often grow on it in dry and hot climates.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sandstone</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SandstoneBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>10</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A brittle yellow stone often found under dry, sandy areas. It can be easily quarried with a pickaxe, and because of its brittleness even with bare hands. It may contain saltpeter, which is found in shallow, flat veins in places where in the ancient times birds nested and covered the ground with their nitrogen rich droppings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sandstone Slab</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SandstoneSlabBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>99</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Slabs are half-height blocks, which can be crafted from a variety of different materials. They can be used to cover floors at a cheaper cost than full size blocks. They can also be used to make long stairs, where each unit of length moves one up by a half unit of height. Slabs can also be affixed to the ceilings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sandstone Stairs</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SandstoneStairsBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>90</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Stairs make it easy to climb slopes. You no longer need to constantly jump and climb vertical faces, instead you move faster, saving time and energy. Stairs can be crafted from various materials. Stairs can also be placed upside-down on the ceilings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sea Urchin</strong></td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sea urchins are small, spiny animals that attach themselves to the sea floor and make a living by filtering nutrients from seawater. They are edible but not very tasty or nutritious.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Seagrass is undersea plant anchored to the sea bottom that reaches up to get access to sunlight. Watch out when swimming because you can get entangled and drown.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

A block of semiconductor.
</div>

Long-sleeved cotton shirt. Can be dyed. Almost no protection. Weak insulation.
</div>

Short pants made of canvas. Can be dyed. Almost no protection. Weak insulation.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Gunpowder keg is a sealed, wooden container filled with gunpowder, with a short fuse attached on the top. The fuse can be lit with a match or initiated electrically. The keg will explode after several seconds with devastating force, destroying blocks and killing any creatures within the blast range. Certain blocks, such as stone, iron and diamond are more resilient to explosions than others. Use detonators to cause an instantaneous explosion without fuse delay.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

Incendiary keg is a gunpowder keg with added incendiary materials. It is weaker than regular gunpowder keg but its debris sets flammable objects on fire. Do not use near forests!
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Snow</strong></td>

A form of frozen water, covers ground in cold biomes. During cold weather, it may fall from the sky as snowfall and cover any area exposed directly to the sky.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Snowballs are made by compacting snow in one's hands. Soft and light, they can be thrown without risk of inflicting damage on the surroundings or injuries in the bystanders. Perfect for fun fights. Be careful when throwing them at dangerous animals, they will usually take a dim view of that.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Socks made of canvas. Can be dyed. Almost no protection. Good insulation.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Soil</strong></td>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Soil is dirt that has been made suitable for cultivation by using a rake. It can be easily damaged by walking or jumping on it. Soil can be fertilized by sprinkling saltpeter on it, plants will grow faster and slowly use up the fertilizer. Soil that is near water will become moist, making plants grow even faster.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A block of coal. If set on fire in the open, it will burn for a very long time. Good as a long lasting fuel in the furnace. You can craft it from a number of coal chunks, or split it back into them. Blocks of coal can sometimes be found underground inside of rich coal seams.
</div>

</div>

A block of diamond. Extremely valuable, contains multiple diamond crystals combined into one large block. Good for decorative purposes. Be careful, because diamond is flammable and will burn when touched by magma or set on fire by a lightning.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sound Generator</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>SoundGeneratorBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>249</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Electric sound generator. Takes 4 inputs: pitch (left), octave (right), volume (top) and tone (bottom). All of these inputs are analog. The pitch ranges from C note (value of 0V) to D note in the next octave (value of 1.4V). 1.5V at pitch input means pause, no sound is played. The octave input can be used to shift the pitch by up to two octaves. The volume input sets the volume of played sound, with 0V being muted and 1.5V being the loudest. The actual sound is played whenever tone input goes from zero to non-zero. The value supplied to the tone input when it goes from zero to non-zero determines which instrument should be played: 0.1=bell, 0.2=Organ, 0.3=Ping, 0.4=String, 0.5=Trumpet, 0.6=Voice, 0.7=Piano, 0.8=PianoSustain, 0.9=Drums, 1.0=Bass. With drums, pitch input determines one of 10 drums: 0.0=Snare, 0.1=BassDrum, 0.2=ClosedHiHat, 0.3=PedalHiHat, 0.4=OpenHiHat, 0.5=LowTom, 0.6=HighTom, 0.7=Crash, 0.8=Ride, 0.9=HandClap. Some animals, especially birds, are afraid of the loud sounds. This element can be placed on any surface and rotated to desired orientation.
</div>

</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sweater made of canvas. Can be dyed. Almost no protection. Good insulation.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
An electric switch. It is used to set the state of an electric circuit to 1.5V (lever up) or 0V (lever down). The voltage remains in the selected state until the lever is moved again. A switch has outputs on all sides for easy connection, and can be placed on any surface.
</div>

Tall grass randomly grows on well lit and humid grass blocks. You can obtain grass seeds from it, which can be used to plant more grass. Can be used as a decorative plant.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Seeds are used to plant various types of plants, such as grass, flowers and rye. Some plants need well tilled, moist and fertilized soil to grow properly. Otherwise their growth will be stunted and they will not reach full size.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Tall spruce leaves grow on tall spruce trees and can be used to obtain tall spruce saplings. They will burn and can be used as a cheap fuel. When placed away from the trunk, they will decay.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A target can be used for shooting practice. It will signal a hit with an analog electric signal. The voltage of the signal determines how close to the center the hit occurred, from 0.8V at the outside of the target to 1.5V at the center. Voltages below 0.8V indicate no hit has been registered.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Thermometer</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>ThermometerBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>187</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Thermometer displays ambient temperature. It is an important tool when planting trees. Oaks like it warm, birches moderately cool and spruces cold. Planting a tree in a wrong ambient temperature will cause the sapling to wilt. It is also an electric element that outputs analog signal corresponding to the surrounding temperature.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

Torch is a piece of flammable coal affixed to a wooden stick. When attached to a wall or placed on the floor, it emits light. It is the easiest and cheapest way to light interiors of buildings or underground corridors.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A programmable circuit that uses a user-entered truth table to determine value of the output for all possible combination of the inputs. There are 4 inputs on all sides and one output at the back (to access the output element must be placed on a wire-through-block). The truth table must be entered manually by tapping edit button while holding the item in hand. Truth table contains output values for all possible input value combinations. Can be placed on any surface and rotated to desired orientation.
</div>

Water is a fluid that easily flows from place to place and fills low-lying areas. A bucket can be used to transport it from a source to a dry area. When placed in a cold area, water will freeze into ice.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Bucket is a container made of copper and used to carry fluids, such as water, magma or milk. Bucket can be filled with the fluid at a source block and transported to a different location, where the source block can be placed to start a new body of fluid. You cannot fill the bucket from a non-source block.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Whistle can be used to summon nearby steeds or drive cattle. Cattle will clump together and move away from the whistling person. You can control them by riding behind and directing them with controlled whistle blows from the sides. Whistle is also perfect for scaring birds.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
White flowers randomly grow on well lit and humid grass blocks. Can be used as a decorative plant.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

Pigment is the main constituent of paints. It can be obtained by firing limestone in the furnace until it turns to white powder. To make white paint, boil the pigment in water using the furnace.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

Wicker lamp is a torch placed inside a wooden and wicker shade. It gives off slightly more light than the pure torch and is a nice, decorative item.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

A glass block can be made stronger and more decorative by framing it in wooden sticks. Perfect for making windows.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

A bricks block with a wire running across a hole drilled through the middle. This block behaves exactly the same as bricks, but can conduct electricity when both ends of the embedded wire are a connected to something. This block can be placed with ends pointing north/south, east/west or up/down. It is very useful when there is a need to run electricity through solid walls, or to insulate the wire from unwanted connections with other wires.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wire Through Cobblestone</strong></td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>

</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A cobblestone block with a wire running across a hole drilled through the middle. This block can conduct electricity when both ends of the embedded wire are a connected to something. It can be placed with ends pointing north/south, east/west or up/down. It is very useful when there is a need to run electricity through solid walls, or to insulate the wire from unwanted connections with other wires.
</div>

A planks block with a wire running across a hole drilled through the middle. This block behaves exactly the same as planks, but can conduct electricity when both ends of the embedded wire are a connected to something. This block can be placed with ends pointing north/south, east/west or up/down. It is very useful when there is a need to run electricity through solid walls, or to insulate the wire from unwanted connections with other wires.
</div>

A semiconductor block with a wire running across a hole drilled through the middle. This block can conduct electricity when both ends of the embedded wire are a connected to something. This block can be placed with ends pointing north/south, east/west or up/down. It is very useful when there is a need to run electricity through solid walls, or to insulate the wire from unwanted connections with other wires.

Makeshift chest armor made of wooden sticks and string. Some protection. No insulation.
</div>

<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A wooden club is a primitive melee weapon, although significantly better than bare hands or a stick. When used frequently, it will quickly get damaged and fail. You should progress to specialized weapons made of metals.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Door</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenDoorBlock</td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Doors are used to make entrances to buildings. They can be opened and closed by clicking on them. When connected to a copper wire, doors will open or close on the rising edge of electric signal. Iron doors cannot be operated manually when electrically connected.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Fence</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenFenceBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>125</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Fences are used to enclose areas and prevent animals from going outside and predators from coming inside. Fence posts can be used as pillars or furniture elements. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Fence Gate</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenFenceGateBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>126</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Gates are used to make entrances to fenced areas. They can be opened and closed by clicking on them. When connected to a copper wire, gates will open or close on the rising edge of electric signal. Animals can't jump a fence gate. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Ladder</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenLadderBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>110</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Ladders are made from sticks or rods. They make it easy to climb and descend tall walls safely. It's safest to climb both up and down facing the wall. That means you have to approach backwards when entering the ladder from the top.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Leggings</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>ClothingBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>162</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>28</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Makeshift legs armor made of wooden sticks and string. Some protection. No insulation.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Rake</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenRakeBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>176</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Rakes are tools for turning dirt into soil. After the soil has been created by a rake, it is ready to be planted. The rake will eventually get damaged and fail. Rakes made of better materials last longer.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Sign</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenPostedSignBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>132</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Signs can contain up to 4 lines of black or colored text. They can be affixed to a vertical surface, placed on a post anchored in the ground, or hung from the ceiling. A sign can be made to act as an internet link by specifying a URL (URLs only work in Adventure mode). When attached to a copper wire, the sign message will be displayed on the screen on the rising edge of an electric signal. Use \ (backslash) to continue message in the same line. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>133</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Slab</strong></td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>94</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Slabs are half-height blocks, which can be crafted from a variety of different materials. They can be used to cover floors at a cheaper cost than full size blocks. They can also be used to make long stairs, where each unit of length moves one up by a half unit of height. Slabs can also be affixed to the ceilings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Spear</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenSpearBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>194</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A spear is a versatile weapon that can be both thrown and used for melee attacks. When used, it will eventually lose its sharpness and fail. Spears made with better materials will last longer and cause more damage. Thrown spears will stick in soft materials. It may be hard to remove a spear which embedded itself too hard.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Stairs</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenStairsBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>85</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Stairs make it easy to climb slopes. You no longer need to constantly jump and climb vertical faces, instead you move faster, saving time and energy. Stairs can be crafted from various materials. Stairs can also be placed upside-down on the ceilings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Trapdoor</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>WoodenTrapdoorBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>108</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Trapdoors are used to make vertical entrances to buildings. They can be opened and closed by clicking on them. When connected to a copper wire, trapdoors will open or close on the rising edge of electric signal. Iron trapdoors cannot be operated manually when electrically connected. They can be placed normally or upside-down, depending whether they are attached to top or bottom side of the block. Upside-down trapdoors will stop falling blocks.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Workbench</strong></td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px; text-align: right;'>CraftingTableBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 80px;'>118</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 80px;'>0</td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Workbench is a piece of workshop furniture made of wood. It is a very important item, because it allows you to craft items that require more than 2x2 crafting slots. All craftable items can be made on it, provided that you have the required materials.
</div>

</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Dirt is a very common block found near the surface in most environments. It can be easily dug even with bare hands, but is best dug with a shovel. When exposed to direct sunlight, grass and flowers will spread to it from nearby blocks, if present.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>6</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SoilBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Soil</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Soil is dirt that has been made suitable for cultivation by using a rake. It can be easily damaged by walking or jumping on it. Soil can be fertilized by sprinkling saltpeter on it, plants will grow faster and slowly use up the fertilizer. Soil that is near water will become moist, making plants grow even faster.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>7</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>LimestoneBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Limestone</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Limestone is a brittle whitish stone occasionally found near the surface close to water bodies. It can be easily quarried with a pickaxe, and because of its brittleness even with bare hands. It can be used for making pigments and marble.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>8</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>GraniteBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Granite</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Granite is a tough grey stone that fills the majority of underground world and makes mountains stand tall. Sometimes contains veins of coal, and, often deeper, copper. When harvested, turns into cobblestone, which can in turn be smelted back into pure granite. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>9</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>BasaltBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Basalt</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Basalt is a very tough dark stone of volcanic origin. To mine it effectively it's best to use a strong pickaxe. Basalt is tougher than granite, and forms the majority of deep underground world. Occasionally has pools of magma embedded in it, so be careful when tunnelling. Veins of sulphur and iron ore might sometimes be found when you are deep enough. Even more rarely, you will find the elusive diamond shining brightly from between the rocks. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>10</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SandstoneBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sandstone</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A brittle yellow stone often found under dry, sandy areas. It can be easily quarried with a pickaxe, and because of its brittleness even with bare hands. It may contain saltpeter, which is found in shallow, flat veins in places where in the ancient times birds nested and covered the ground with their nitrogen rich droppings. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>11</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>GravelBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Gravel</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Gravel is an aggregate of fine stones. Caches of gravel are often found underground, and it sometimes covers beaches. It is then known under the name of shingle. Gravel is a loose material that will collapse when tunnelled under, and may easily bury the unwary, so be careful. It is the best source of rocks for throwing.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>12</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>ClayBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Clay</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Clay is a heavy, densely packed soil made of very small grains. From time immemorial it was known that by firing it in a furnace one can obtain bricks, which are the most versatile building material. Clay is often found on the bottoms of shallow lakes. Can be painted.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>13</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SandBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sand</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sand is a yellowish loose material that covers desert areas and beaches. Be careful because sand will collapse when tunnelled under. It is most commonly used to make glass. Cacti will often grow on it in dry and hot climates.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>14</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>GrassBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Grass</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Grass grows on dirt blocks when there is enough sunlight available. When conditions are right, various flowers, tall grass blades and vegetables will grow on it. Grass is easily destroyed by covering it with something heavy and takes a long time to regrow.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>15</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>WaterBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Water</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Water is a fluid that easily flows from place to place and fills low-lying areas. A bucket can be used to transport it from a source to a dry area. When placed in a cold area, water will freeze into ice.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>16</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>MagmaBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Magma</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Hot molten rock occasionally found in pools, deep underground in basalt. It is extremely hot and will set anything flammable on fire as soon as it touches it. It will also very quickly kill any living thing that happens to fall into it. A bucket can be used to transport it away from its source.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>17</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SnowBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Snow</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A form of frozen water, covers ground in cold biomes. During cold weather, it may fall from the sky as snowfall and cover any area exposed directly to the sky.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>18</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>IceBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Ice</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A block of water which has frozen solid. Whenever water is exposed to low temperatures, it will turn into ice. Ice can in turn be melted back into water by using fire or magma as a heat source. It has a slippery surface that makes walking difficult.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Fire</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; color: #666;'>
<em>No description available</em>
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Stone Chunk</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A stone can be a formidable weapon in the hands of a skilled thrower. They are plentiful and easy to obtain. The best stones are found by digging in gravel blocks.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>22</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>CoalOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Coal Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Coal ore is a vein of coal embedded in granite rock. Gains coal chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>23</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>CopperOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Copper Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Copper ore is a vein of green copper carbonate embedded in granite rock. Gains malachite chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost. Further processing in the furnace is required to extract pure copper from the carbonate.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>24</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>IronOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Iron Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Iron ore is a vein of iron oxide embedded in basalt rock. Gains iron ore chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost. Further processing in the furnace is required to extract pure iron from the oxide.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>25</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SulphurOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Sulphur Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sulphur ore is a vein of elemental sulphur embedded in basalt rock. Gains sulphur chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost. Sulphur can be used to make pyrotechnic substances, like gunpowder or matches.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>26</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>DiamondOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Diamond Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Diamond ore are diamond crystals embedded in basalt rock. Gains diamonds when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>27</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>GermaniumOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Germanium Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Germanium ore are veins of germanium oxide embedded in basalt rock. Gains germanium ore blocks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>28</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>SaltpeterOreBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Saltpeter Ore</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Saltpeter ore is a vein of potassium nitrate embedded in sandstone rock. Gains saltpeter chunks when mined. Be careful, because certain ores require special tools for successful mining of the mineral. If improper tools are used, the mineral is lost. Saltpeter can be used to make explosives.
</div>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A block of coal. If set on fire in the open, it will burn for a very long time. Good as a long lasting fuel in the furnace. You can craft it from a number of coal chunks, or split it back into them. Blocks of coal can sometimes be found underground inside of rich coal seams.
</div>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A chunk of pure coal. Can be used for fuelling the furnace and as an ingredient of various substances, like gunpowder. Used as a reagent when reducing metal ores to pure metals. Also used as a pigment for making darker hues of paint. In absence of mineral coal, you can burn wood in an oxygen-poor atmosphere in the furnace and obtain a similar substance: charcoal.
</div>

A chunk of copper carbonate with beautiful green color. When smelted in the furnace will turn into pure copper. Multiple chunks can be combined and form a large block of malachite, a beautiful decorative material.
</div>

</div>

A chunk of potassium nitrate. This is a white substance with high oxygen content that can be used as an oxidizer for making explosives or, when sprinkled over soil, as a fertilizer to speed up growth of plants and increase yields.
</div>

</div>

Germanium crystal is a crystal of pure electronic-grade germanium. It is a semiconductor and can be used to make electronic logic gates.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Diamond is a crystal of pure carbon. Very rare and beautiful, it is found deep underground. It can be used for making extremely hard and durable tools. Being carbon, can also be burned as fuel in the furnace, but only fools waste this precious substance for such a mundane purpose.
</div>

Oak wood grows in trunks of oak trees, which grow in warm and humid areas. It can be crafted into planks, which are a versatile building and crafting material. It can also be used as a fuel in the furnace. To replant trees you need to obtain saplings from the leaves.
</div>

</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Plant rye on tilled, moist and fertilized soil to obtain full grown plants. Harvesting the plants will yield large number of rye seeds. Rye also grows naturally in form or wild rye. You can obtain wild seeds by harvesting it.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Saplings are used to plant trees. You can obtain them by crafting from the leaves of trees. When planting, be careful to place the saplings in a well lit place, full of moisture and of correct temperature. Use hygrometer and thermometer to measure these quantities. Oaks like it warm, birches moderately cool and spruces cold. All trees need a lot of humidity. If you plant a sapling in a wrong spot, it will wilt into a dry bush.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Cacti are large plants very tolerant to dry conditions, and will often be found growing to large sizes in the desert. They are prickly and cause injuries on touch, so be careful. They will regrow to up to 3 blocks high when only the top part is harvested. A cruel hunter may line their trap pit with these for a more effective kill.
</div>

Cotton is a plant that grows in warm areas. When harvested, provides a number of cotton wads which can be further processed into cotton products.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wooden Ladder</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Ladders are made from sticks or rods. They make it easy to climb and descend tall walls safely. It's safest to climb both up and down facing the wall. That means you have to approach backwards when entering the ladder from the top.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>IronLadderBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Iron Ladder</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Ladders are made from sticks or rods. They make it easy to climb and descend tall walls safely. It's safest to climb both up and down facing the wall. That means you have to approach backwards when entering the ladder from the top.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Wicker Lamp</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Wicker lamp is a torch placed inside a wooden and wicker shade. It gives off slightly more light than the pure torch and is a nice, decorative item.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>113</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>TorchBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Torch</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Torch is a piece of flammable coal affixed to a wooden stick. When attached to a wall or placed on the floor, it emits light. It is the easiest and cheapest way to light interiors of buildings or underground corridors.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>114</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>StickBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Stick</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Sticks are made by cutting wooden planks into pieces. They are a versatile building material used in construction of many items, such as torches, ladders or fences. They can also be used as a last resort weapon or tool, although their attack power is low.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Iron Ingot</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
An ingot of pure iron. Can be crafted into very durable and strong iron tools. Only diamond-edged tools can best them. Combine multiple ingots to form an iron block.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Copper Ingot</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
An ingot of pure copper. Can be crafted into copper tools, such as buckets, wires and other electric devices. Combine multiple ingots to form a copper block.
</div>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Rods are made by forging iron into shape. They are useful for making fences, doors and other things.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Workbench</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Workbench is a piece of workshop furniture made of wood. It is a very important item, because it allows you to craft items that require more than 2x2 crafting slots. All craftable items can be made on it, provided that you have the required materials.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>FurnaceBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Furnace</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A furnace is an enclosure made from fire resistant stone, in which a high temperature is maintained by burning combustible fuel. It is an important item that allows smelting, cooking and other forms of heat processing. The best fuel for the furnace are coal chunks, but wood and even leaves can be used if coal is unavailable. Furnace can be left unattended while working. It also emits a small amount of light.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Furnace</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>

A chest made of wood is a good place to store excess items that you can't or don't want to carry. It will hold up to 16 stacks of items.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Dispenser</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A dispenser stores items in a similar way to a chest, but allows dispensing them through the hole when triggered via rising edge of an electric signal. It can either dispense the item or shoot it as projectile, depending on the setting.
</div>

Wooden spikes penetrate flesh. The spiked planks can be placed on the floor, on the walls and on the ceiling. Touching them will cause injury. Trap pits are often lined with them. Spikes can be electrically controlled and will extend/retract on a rising edge of an electric signal.
</div>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Grass trap is a deception device that resembles grass but collapses when something heavy stands on it. It's used for concealing traps so that an animal, unwary of the trap will walk on it and fall into the pit. It can only be crafted from oak leaves, no other leaves will work.
</div>

</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Fences are used to enclose areas and prevent animals from going outside and predators from coming inside. Fence posts can be used as pillars or furniture elements. Can be painted.
</div>

Gates are used to make entrances to fenced areas. They can be opened and closed by clicking on them. When connected to a copper wire, gates will open or close on the rising edge of electric signal. Animals can't jump a fence gate. Can be painted.
</div>

Signs can contain up to 4 lines of black or colored text. They can be affixed to a vertical surface, placed on a post anchored in the ground, or hung from the ceiling. A sign can be made to act as an internet link by specifying a URL (URLs only work in Adventure mode). When attached to a copper wire, the sign message will be displayed on the screen on the rising edge of an electric signal. Use \ (backslash) to continue message in the same line. Can be painted.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Gunpowder keg is a sealed, wooden container filled with gunpowder, with a short fuse attached on the top. The fuse can be lit with a match or initiated electrically. The keg will explode after several seconds with devastating force, destroying blocks and killing any creatures within the blast range. Certain blocks, such as stone, iron and diamond are more resilient to explosions than others. Use detonators to cause an instantaneous explosion without fuse delay.
</div>

</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Campfire is easy to construct, provides heat, light and keeps predators away. You can make it bigger by throwing combustible items on top, such as sticks, leaves, wood blocks, planks etc.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>149</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>GunpowderBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Gunpowder</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
A substance capable of burning in an oxygenless environment because it contains its own source of oxygen, saltpeter. When burning, gunpowder emits large amounts of hot gases. Will explode with devastating force if confined in a small volume, such as a keg, and ignited.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 100px; '>150</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; font-family: monospace; font-size: 12px;'>PigmentBlock</td>
<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>White Pigment</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Pigment is the main constituent of paints. It can be obtained by firing limestone in the furnace until it turns to white powder. To make white paint, boil the pigment in water using the furnace.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Jack O'Lantern</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Jack O'Lanterns are usually carved from ripe pumpkins during Halloween. They emit weak light.
</div>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; text-align: center; width: 100px;'>0</td>

<td style='padding: 8px 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'><strong>Magnet</strong></td>
</tr>
</table>
<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Magnets attract compass needle allowing you to find direction home while out in the wild. Don't forget to place one close to your house! The compass will show direction to the nearest magnet, or to spawn location if there are no magnets. You can only place a limited number of magnets in the world.
</div>

</tr>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>

</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Leather can be obtained by killing certain animals. It is a versatile material that can be used as a extremely durable, wear and tear resistant and waterproof fabric. It is also used to make saddles.
</div>

Spawner egg. Bird eggs are occasionally laid by adult birds. If you look carefully, you may find and eat them. Cooking an egg will increase its nutritional value. Non-bird eggs are available in creative mode only and can be used to spawn all other animals. To spawn an animal in creative mode, throw the egg against a hard surface to smash it. Spawner eggs of some exotic animals are unavailable even in creative mode.
</div>

Stone axe is a primitive tool that can equally be used for digging, cutting wood and quarrying rocks. It greatly speeds up the process, but it will quickly get damaged and fail. Stone axe is a poor melee weapon, make a club if you want to fight. You should progress to specialized tools made of metals.
</div>

</div>

A piece of sulphur on a stick, can be used to start fires. Be very careful when walking with matches because it is very easy to accidentally start a fire that may go out of control. Can be used to ignite fuses and as a weapon to set other animals on fire.
</div>

<div style='background-color: #fafbfc; padding: 12px; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8; border-top: none; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5;'>
Compass points to the location of the place of last sleep or to the nearest magnet, if present. It is indispensable when finding your way home on far away journeys.
</div>
<table style='width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;'>
<tr style='background-color: #f6f8fa; border: 1px solid #e1e4e8;'>
